# Skills · P · part 147

820 entries, property-describe to propose-change-pr. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **property-describe**: Use when Obsidian vault notes need AI-generated description properties in their YAML frontmatter. Trigger phrases - "add descriptions", "fill descriptions", "generate summaries", "description property", "empty description", "missing description". Also trigger when notes have placeholder descriptions (TBD, TODO) or when batch-filling descriptions across a folder. This is a token-intensive operation (reads full note content) — run it deliberately, not as part of every property pass. ([neckarshore-skills/obsidian-vault-autopilot](https://github.com/neckarshore-skills/obsidian-vault-autopilot/tree/HEAD/skills/property-describe) · ★3)

([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/property-description) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **property-developer**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a property/real estate developer — underwriting a land acquisition against residual land value, structuring an entitlement-contingent option deal, building or stress-testing a development pro forma, sizing a capital stack across debt and equity, or deciding whether to proceed, renegotiate, or walk on a deal after a construction bid or zoning setback. - **property-dispute**: [Property] 物业纠纷全流程维权助手。业主描述物业问题（电梯故障/漏水/物业费涨价/账目不透明/公共收益被侵占/服务不达标等），自动完成权利评估-法律条款速查-升级流程导航-投诉文件生成-谈判脚本输出-公共税务审计6大阶段分析，生成交互式HTML可视化维权报告。覆盖民法典物权编/物业服务合同/侵权责任、物业管理条例、消防法、税收征管法等法律法规，提供住建局/市场监管局/税务局/消防等多部门联合举报策略。触发词：物业纠纷, 物业问题, 电梯坏了, 漏水, 物业费涨价, 物业费涨了, 物业不管, 物业维权, 投诉物业, 业主维权, 物业投诉, 物业不作为, 天花板漏水, 公共收益, 物业查账, property dispute, 物业起诉, 退物业费, 解聘物业, 物业财务, 物业账目。 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/property-dispute) · ★37)
- **property-division**: 夫妻财产分割——共同财产/个人财产认定 + 分割方案 + 特殊财产处理。 适用情形:离婚财产分割 / 婚内财产约定 / 离婚后财产纠纷。 核心:财产性质认定 + 分割原则 + 特殊财产(房产/股权/古董/数字资产)。 ([vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal](https://github.com/vivy-yi/Greater-China-Legal/tree/HEAD/plugins/legal-scenes/family-law/skills/property-division) · ★50)
